Corynebacterium diphtheriae

NCBITaxon:1717 · SPECIES · BACTERIA · SEEDED · grounding EXACT

Lineage: root › cellular organisms › Bacteria › Bacillati › Actinomycetota › Actinomycetes › Mycobacteriales › Corynebacteriaceae › Corynebacterium

Synonyms: Bacillus diphtheriae (NCBITaxon); Bacterium diphtheriae (NCBITaxon); Microsporon diphthericum (NCBITaxon); Microsporon diphtheriticum (NCBITaxon); Mycobacterium diphtheriae (NCBITaxon); Pacinia loeffleri (NCBITaxon)

Strains

168 BacDive strains classified here.

NCBI assemblies appear first, followed by GTDB, BV-BRC/PATRIC, IMG and AllTheBacteria genome records. Links retain evidence from BacDive, GTDB metadata, GOLD and StrainInfo; AllTheBacteria links preserve the BioSample evidence connecting the assembly to the strain. Supplied accession versions, source strain identifiers and database meanings are preserved. NCBI accessions without a version suffix have unspecified version. Links under one strain do not establish genome equivalence; source descriptions may identify a substrain or plasmid. Samples, GOLD organism/project records, StrainInfo strain/deposit records and nucleotide references are listed separately. StrainInfo sequences must explicitly name the matched source deposit. The complete NCBI assembly inventory, other genome-record inventory, related-record inventory AllTheBacteria browser and StrainInfo browser include strains omitted from this listing.
